### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- **rsft-early-scorefilter-validation Worker 回调** → 确认 score_filter 能正常处理 generate 输出。如有问题，立即派 Worker 修 bug（generate 还在跑，来得及）

- **RSFT 训练完成** → eval 对比。pass criteria: rel_f1 > SFT baseline (0.406)。如 pass → complete_mvp()→ paper_framing.md → experiment_plan.yaml
- **RSFT 失败** → 按 ROADMAP contingency: (1) DPO+CED 并行备选; (2) 全参微调排除 QLoRA 根因; (3) CED 作为推理时 reranker

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **核心方向**: FREIGE = CED reward 训练 LLM 做文档级关系抽取 + 可验证证据生成
- **MVP-1 已通过**: CED margin=0.4827, accuracy=81.5%, r=0.34 → CED reward signal 有效
- **MVP-2 当前 pivot**: 在线 RL (GRPO) 失败 → 离线 RL (RSFT)。CED signal 本身没问题，问题是 GRPO+QLoRA 作为优化方法论不适合结构化生成
- **SFT baseline**: rel_f1=0.406, evi_f1=0.628 — 这是 RSFT 必须超越的 bar
- **DPO trainer 代码已准备好**（worker_code_dpo-trainer-prep 完成），作为 RSFT 的并行备选。RSFT 优先是因为最简单最稳定
- **容易判断错的事**: (1) 不要因为 cuda:0/2/3 空闲就急着跑新 GRPO 实验——方向已判死; (2) RSFT generate 输出在 `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/`，score_filter 的 --input_dir 指向它; (3) 3 个 PREPARING 实验是废弃残留，不要尝试重启

      "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
      "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
      "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
      "- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"

{
  "parentUuid": "d96e2763-6e5b-47ee-8584-a9dcaa016444",
  "isSidechain": false,
  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

EXIT_CODE=129 is artifact of KL early stop exit path.\\\\n\\\\nerror_type: hyperparameter | can_auto_fix: false\\\\nFix options: (1) add --gradient_accumulation_steps 16 for effective batch ~16; (2) reduce lr from 5e-5 to 5e-6; (3) increase kl_coef from 0.05 to 0.5; (4) lr warmup steps\\\"}, \\\"mvp2_grpo_ced_r2\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T10:58:11.663Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T11:31:49.724Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"killed\\\"}, \\\"mvp2_grpo_ced_r1\\\": {\\\"status\\\": \\\"DONE\\\", \\\"created_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T08:02:41.858Z\\\", \\\"updated_at\\\": \\\"2026-05-14T10:37:23.856Z\\\", \\\"conclusion\\\": \\\"killed\\\", \\\"notes\\\": \\\"Mode collapse diagnosed at step 80/189 (F1=0.001, format reward hacking). Root causes and fixes applied to grpo_trainer.py:\\\\n\\\\n(A) Checkpoint: save_strategy was \\\\\\\"epoch\\\\\\\" → now supports --save_steps/--save_total_limit CLI args, defaults to steps-based saving. Validated: checkpoint-1/2 saved in dry-run.\\\\n\\\\n(B) Reward weighting: all 3 rewards (format/f1/ced) were equally summed by TRL → added WeightedRewardFn wrapper + --format_reward_weight/--f1_reward_weight/--ced_reward_weight CLI args. Dry-run confirmed weighting works (format raw=0.1 × w=0.3 → 0.03).\\\\n\\\\n(C) Clip ratio: num_iterations=1 (default) meant single pass, ratio≡1, clipping never activated → added --num_iterations CLI arg (set to 2 in r2). The problem is cuda:1's process was launched without shard arguments, so it'll continue processing the entire range rather than stopping at its assigned boundary.

I need to spawn a Worker to handle this properly—it should SSH into the server, check cuda:1's current progress, kill the full-range process, and restart all four GPUs with their respective shard assignments.

The key issue is that killing cuda:1 means losing already-generated output, so I should first check what's been written to disk and have the new shards skip those samples. I can just divide the dataset into 4 equal parts and submit jobs for the other GPUs without waiting for reconnaissance.

The issue is that cuda:1's existing process is running the full range (0-3053), not just its shard (0-763), so it'll keep generating duplicates. I'm weighing three options: kill and restart everything cleanly but lose progress, let cuda:1 continue on the full range while the other shards handle 763+ (accepting some duplication), or start the other shards and have a Worker monitor for completion and deduplication.

Looking at the math, cuda:1 has only completed about 7% of the work so far (112 samples out of ~1527 batches), and since the output is per-sample, the shard mechanism operates at the sample level. The cleanest approach is to kill the current cuda:1 process since it's barely made progress, then restart all four shards with proper boundaries.

But actually, I could just let cuda:0/2/3 start immediately on their respective shards while cuda:1 continues—the skip-existing logic should handle any overlaps. This way the idle GPUs aren't wasting time while I wait for the Worker to analyze the situation.

Let me figure out what the original command looked like. I know the generate script takes --shard_id, --start_idx, and --end_idx parameters, so I can reconstruct a reasonable submit_training_job call for the three remaining shards without needing the exact original spec. I'll spawn a Worker to SSH into the server and check the RSFT generation code for shard arguments, review the current progress on cuda:1, and get the total sample count so I can submit three new training jobs with the exact commands needed.

{
  "task_type": "ssh:rsft-parallel-recon",
  "project": "faith-evidence-ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：快速侦查 RSFT 生成并行化所需信息\n\n你需要 SSH 到服务器，收集以下信息后立即汇报。**纯 read_only 操作，不要修改任何东西。**\n\n### 服务器\n- 服务器名: rtx5090-4x\n- 工作目录: /root/autodl-tmp/\n- 代码: /root/autodl-tmp/freige/rsft_generate.py\n\n### 需要收集的信息\n\n**1. rsft_generate.py 的 shard/分片参数**\n```bash\n# 查看脚本的 argparse 参数，找到 shard 相关的参数名\ngrep -n \"add_argument.*shard\\|add_argument.*start\\|add_argument.*end\\|add_argument.*split\" /root/autodl-tmp/freige/rsft_generate.py\n# 如果上面没找到，看完整的 argparse 定义\ngrep -n \"add_argument\" /root/autodl-tmp/freige/rsft_generate.py\n```\n\n**2. 当前 cuda:1 生成进度**\n```bash\n# 查看已生成的文件数量和格式\nls /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/ | head -20\nls /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/ | wc -l\n# 如果是单个文件(jsonl)，查看行数\nwc -l /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/*.jsonl 2>/dev/null\n# 查看 tmux 日志最后几行看进度\ntmux capture-pane -t mvp2_rsft_generate -p | tail -20\n```\n\n**3. 数据集总样本数确认**\n```bash\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && python -c \"\nfrom datasets import load_dataset\nds = load_dataset('json', data_files='/root/autodl-tmp/data/docred/train_annotated.json')\nprint(f'Total samples: {len(ds[\\\"train\\\"])}')\n\" 2>/dev/null || wc -l /root/autodl-tmp/data/docred/train_annotated.json\n```\n\n**4. 当前进程状态**\n```bash\nnvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,memory.used,utilization.gpu --format=csv,noheader\nps aux | grep rsft_generate | grep -v grep\n```\n\n**5. 原始启动命令**（从 tmux 或 bash_history 找）\n```bash\ntmux list-sessions 2>/dev/null\n# 尝试从 cron 或 history 找到原始命令\ncat /root/.bash_history | grep rsft_generate | tail -5\n```\n\n### 汇报格式\n请输出：\n1. shard 参数名列表（--start_idx? --shard_id? --end_idx?）和每个参数的含义\n2. 已生成多少样本/文件\n3. 总样本数\n4. 当前 GPU 使用情况\n5. 原始启动命令（如果找到）\n6. 建议的 3 个 shard 命令（cuda:0/2/3），包含完整参数\n\n速度优先，2-3 轮 SSH 调用即可完成。",
